Pack 1 · Contextual questions

29 words · 116 sentences · 232 questions · Four choices each

208 new questions await text approval; 24 approved questions are included for reference. Audio follows text approval.

116 sentence contexts · 232 questions shown

Please refer to the sentence/question IDs when suggesting edits. Readable Markdown · Genki I scope · Issue #183

Coverage and progression notes

WaniKani level 1: two simple and two contrastive variants per word, each with a target-answer and an other-detail question. Review Japanese, readings, English, correct answers and distractors. Pictures are proposed only where all four choices have suitable approved artwork. The 104 new contexts remain pending; twelve existing contexts retain their approved identities and answers.

Contextual only: 下さい・力いっぱい・大した. These need direct entry into simple contextual practice in a later integration. 人工・人口 retain word cards, with homophones excluded as standalone distractors. The seven bare numerals and ふじ山 remain word-first. This text review does not generate audio, activate cards or save progress.

Simple

1. 上 — Above

ja-ctx-simple-ue-01 · revision 1 · target ja-v-c0f58bfab324

Existing approved text · Existing media preserved

本は机の上にあります。

ほんはつくえのうえにあります。

The book is on the desk.

Grammar and supporting words

Noun linking and possession with の (Genki I 1); Core particles and word order (Genki I 3); Existence and relative location with あります/います (Genki I 4)

上 (うえ: on; above); 本 (ほん: book); 机 (つくえ: desk)

Target occurrence: 上 (うえ)

Review notes: One short location fact introduces direct questions before the matching contrastive context. Existing 上/下 pictures show Kiku on/under a chair, and left/right use arrows; owner review should confirm that these position concepts transfer clearly to the desk sentence.

Answer is the target

3. 上 — Above

ja-ctx-ue-01 · revision 1 · target ja-v-c0f58bfab324

Existing approved text · Existing media preserved

本は机の上に、ノートは机の下にあります。

ほんはつくえのうえに、ノートはつくえのしたにあります。

The book is on the desk, and the notebook is under the desk.

Grammar and supporting words

Noun linking and possession with の (Genki I 1); Core particles and word order (Genki I 3); Existence and relative location with あります/います (Genki I 4)

上 (うえ: on; above); 下 (した: under; below); 本 (ほん: book); ノート (ノート: notebook); 机 (つくえ: desk)

Target occurrence: 上 (うえ)

Review notes: The 上/下 pictures show Kiku on/under a chair; left/right are arrows. They stand for relative-position concepts, not the literal desk or shelf scene. Owner review must confirm this transfer is clear. The question binds one object to one location.

Answer is the target

5. 大人 — Adult

ja-ctx-wk1-simple-otona-01 · revision 1 · target ja-v-2cc50c2d66a6

Text: pending · Audio: not-generated

部屋には大人が二人います。

へやにはおとながふたりいます。

There are two adults in the room.

Grammar and supporting words

Core particles and word order (Genki I 3); Existence and relative location with あります/います (Genki I 4); Counting people (Genki I 7)

大人 (おとな: adult); 子供 (こども: child); 二人 (ふたり: two people); 三人 (さんにん: three people); 部屋 (へや: room); 教室 (きょうしつ: classroom); 公園 (こうえん: park); 店 (みせ: shop); 誰 (だれ: who); 何人 (なんにん: how many people); どこ (どこ: where); 子供が二人 (こどもがふたり: two children); 大人が三人 (おとながさんにん: three adults); 子供が三人 (こどもがさんにん: three children); 大人が二人 (おとながふたり: two adults)

Target occurrence: 大人 (おとな)

Review notes: Phrase choices distinguish age group and count; no generic 人 or gender labels compete with 大人.

Answer is the target

Grammar and supporting words

Core particles and word order (Genki I 3); Action in progress with ている (Genki I 7); Polite present verb forms (Genki I 3)

大人 (おとな: adult); 子供 (こども: child); 二人 (ふたり: two people); 三人 (さんにん: three people); 部屋 (へや: room); 教室 (きょうしつ: classroom); 公園 (こうえん: park); 店 (みせ: shop); 誰 (だれ: who); 何人 (なんにん: how many people); どこ (どこ: where); 本 (ほん: book); 読む (よむ: read); 走る (はしる: run); 寝る (ねる: sleep); 食べる (たべる: to eat); 何 (なに: what); 犬 (いぬ: dog); 猫 (ねこ: cat); 走っています (はしっています: is running); 寝ています (ねています: is sleeping); 本を読んでいます (ほんをよんでいます: is reading a book); 食べています (たべています: is eating)

Target occurrence: 大人 (おとな)

Review notes: Adult/child distinction; dog/cat choices are named animals, not overlapping human categories.

Answer is the target

Contrastive

7. 大人 — Adult

ja-ctx-wk1-contrastive-otona-01 · revision 1 · target ja-v-2cc50c2d66a6

Text: pending · Audio: not-generated

部屋には大人が二人います。公園には子供が三人います。

へやにはおとながふたりいます。こうえんにはこどもがさんにんいます。

There are two adults in the room. There are three children in the park.

兄 (あに: my older brother); 妹 (いもうと: my younger sister); 友達 (ともだち: friend); 母 (はは: my mother); 父 (ちち: my father); 映画 (えいが: movie); テレビ (テレビ: television); 写真 (しゃしん: photograph); 地図 (ちず: map); 見る (みる: to watch; to see); 誰 (だれ: who); 何 (なに: what); 何人 (なんにん: how many people); 母と (ははと: with my mother); 父と (ちちと: with my father); 一人で (ひとりで: alone); 妹と (いもうとと: with my younger sister)

Target occurrence: 一人 (ひとり)

Review notes: This variant teaches 一人で (alone/by oneself), separately from the one-person counting use. 一人で naturally answers the question about a companion by explaining that he watches by himself.

ノートは椅子の下に、本は椅子の上にあります。

ノートはいすのしたに、ほんはいすのうえにあります。

The notebook is under the chair, and the book is on the chair.

Grammar and supporting words

Noun linking and possession with の (Genki I 1); Core particles and word order (Genki I 3); Existence and relative location with あります/います (Genki I 4)

上 (うえ: on; above); 下 (した: under; below); 本 (ほん: book); ノート (ノート: notebook); 椅子 (いす: chair)

Target occurrence: 下 (した)

Review notes: The 上/下 pictures show Kiku on/under a chair; left/right are arrows. They stand for relative-position concepts, not the literal desk or shelf scene. Owner review must confirm this transfer is clear. The question binds one object to one location.

Answer is the target

Grammar and supporting words
